      SimulationStorage         SimulationStorage                                             Type: Enumeration  #Vals:  1  Units:                   WriteSimFlag: 1  WriteXMLArrayIndices: F
                                      default:  1
                                            0:  "0 - ALL simulation sub-directories and files deleted"
                                            1:  "1 - Only the input (.idf) files are retained (user default)"
                                            2:  "2 - (#1 above) + summary output (.htm) retained"
                                            3:  "3 - (#2 above) + limited additional output (.csv|.eio|.err|.rdd) retained"
                                            4:  "4 - (#3 above) + SQL output (.sql) retained"
                                            5:  "5 - (#3 (not 4) above) + other standard output (.eso|.mtr) retained"
                                            6:  "6 - (#4 & 5 above) - both SQL & other standard output files retained"
                                            7:  "7 - ALL simulation input and output files retained"
      AnalysisStorage           AnalysisStorage                                               Type: Enumeration  #Vals:  1  Units:                   WriteSimFlag: 1  WriteXMLArrayIndices: F
                                      default:  2
                                            0:  "0 - ALL analysis files deleted (along w/ processing directory if no other sim/csv files stored)"
                                            1:  "1 - Only simulation SDD XML (.xml) files are retained"
                                            2:  "2 - (#1 above) + OpenStudio model (.osm) files are retained (user default)"
                                            3:  "3 - ALL files produced during analysis are retained"
